- **lint-router**: A registry-driven lint router: it picks which linters to run based on WHO the work is for — matched by git origin or marker files — then runs that profile's linters on just the files you changed. A profile can layer a linter as an overlay on a repo's own config or run one standalone, and a profile that matches nothing runs nothing — so it never imposes a lint on code… ([shawnroos/shrimpshack](https://github.com/shawnroos/shrimpshack/tree/HEAD/plugins/lint-router) · 6 skills, 1 hook file)
